## קָצִין (id: BDB07226)

\[Str6278] † קָצִין \[עֵה] **n.pr.loc.** on border of Zebulun, only c. ה loc., ´עִתָּה ק [Jos 19:13](https://ref.ly/Josh19:13) site unknown.

> \[Str6258,2088,2009,5704,3588] עַתָּ֫ה:435 (also Ez [23:43](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:43), [Psalm 74:6](https://ref.ly/Ps74:6) עת Kt, Qr עַתָּה), עָ֫תָּה [Gn 32:5](https://ref.ly/Gen32:5) \+, **adv.** of time, now (prop. acc. of \[ï*int*], עֵת (Köii. 260\), *at the time*, in partic. of the present time, i.e. *now;* cf. Germ. *zur Zeit*, Ar. اَلْآنَ *at the time*, i.e. *now);—now:* **1\. a.** [Gn 12:19](https://ref.ly/Gen12:19) ועתה הנה אשׁתך and *now*, behold thy wife, [22:12](https://ref.ly/Gen22:12) כי עתח ידעתי *now*, I know (so [Ex 18:11](https://ref.ly/Exod18:11), Ju [17:13](https://ref.ly/Judg17:13), [1 K 17:24](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:24) \[עַתָּה זֶה], [Psalm 20:7](https://ref.ly/Ps20:7)), [26:22](https://ref.ly/Gen26:22), [26:29](https://ref.ly/Gen26:29), [27:36](https://ref.ly/Gen27:36), [31:13](https://ref.ly/Gen31:13), [31:28](https://ref.ly/Gen31:28), etc., [Ex 5:5](https://ref.ly/Exod5:5) הך רביס עתה עס הארץ; whether in opp. to past time, as [Gn 32:11](https://ref.ly/Gen32:11), [Jos 14:11](https://ref.ly/Josh14:11) כְּכֹחִי אָז וּכְכֹחִי עָתָּה, Ju [11:8](https://ref.ly/Judg11:8), [1 K 12:4](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:4), [Is 1:21](https://ref.ly/Isa1:21) ועתה מרצחים, [16:14](https://ref.ly/Isa16:14), [Ho 2:9](https://ref.ly/Hos2:9) כִּי טוֺב לִי אָז מַעָתָּה, [13:2](https://ref.ly/Hos13:2), [Jb 30:1](https://ref.ly/Job30:1) \+, or to future, as [Nu 24:17](https://ref.ly/Num24:17) אֶרְאֶנּוּ ולא עתה, Ju [8:6](https://ref.ly/Judg8:6) חֲכַף עַתָּה־כְּיָדְֶָ ´זֶבַח וצ, [1 S 2:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:16) כִּי עַתָּה תִּתֵּן; \= *after all* [Nu 22:38](https://ref.ly/Num22:38), Ju [8:2](https://ref.ly/Judg8:2); \= *now at last* [2 S 24:16](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:16), [1 K 19:4](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:4), [2 K 19:25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:25), [Ho 7:2](https://ref.ly/Hos7:2), [Je 4:12](https://ref.ly/Jer4:12); and in the phr. מעתה ועד עולם † [Is 9:6](https://ref.ly/Isa9:6), [59:21](https://ref.ly/Isa59:21), [Mi 4:7](https://ref.ly/Mic4:7) (v. infr.), [Psalm 113:2](https://ref.ly/Ps113:2), [115:18](https://ref.ly/Ps115:18), [121:8](https://ref.ly/Ps121:8), [125:2](https://ref.ly/Ps125:2), [131:3](https://ref.ly/Ps131:3). **b.** of the imminent or impending future: [Gn 19:9](https://ref.ly/Gen19:9) עַתָּה נָרַע לְ מַהֶם. [29:32](https://ref.ly/Gen29:32) כי עתה יאהבני אישׁי, v:[34](https://ref.ly/Gen29:34), [Ex 6:1](https://ref.ly/Exod6:1) עתה תראה, [Nu 11:23](https://ref.ly/Num11:23), [22:4](https://ref.ly/Num22:4), [2 S 20:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:6), [Is 33:10](https://ref.ly/Isa33:10) ([Psalm 12:6](https://ref.ly/Ps12:6)), [43:19](https://ref.ly/Isa43:19), [49:19](https://ref.ly/Isa49:19), [Am 6:7](https://ref.ly/Amos6:7) לכן עתה יִגְלוּ, [Is 49:19](https://ref.ly/Isa49:19), [Mi 7:4](https://ref.ly/Mic7:4), [7:10](https://ref.ly/Mic7:10), [Dn 10:20](https://ref.ly/Dan10:20); and esp. ἀσυνδέτως in Hos, introducing a punishment, [Ho 4:16](https://ref.ly/Hos4:16) ´וגו ´עתה יִרְעֵם י [5:7](https://ref.ly/Hos5:7), [8:8](https://ref.ly/Hos8:8), [8:13](https://ref.ly/Hos8:13) ([Je 14:10](https://ref.ly/Jer14:10)), [10:2](https://ref.ly/Hos10:2). **c.** of a time *ideally* present (\= *then*, from our point of view): [Is 29:22](https://ref.ly/Isa29:22) לא עתה יבושׁ יעקב, vb, [Ho 10:3](https://ref.ly/Hos10:3), [Mi 4:9](https://ref.ly/Mic4:9), [4:10](https://ref.ly/Mic4:10), [4:11](https://ref.ly/Mic4:11), [4:14](https://ref.ly/Mic4:14), [5:3](https://ref.ly/Mic5:3). **d.** describing a present state \= *as things are:* [1 S 8:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam8:5) thou art old, etc., עתה שִימָה לנו מלך, [9:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam9:6), [13:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:12), [14:30](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:30), [25:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:7) b, [27:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam27:1) *now* I shall be swept away one day, etc., [2 K 18:20](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:20), [18:25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:25), [Jb 6:21](https://ref.ly/Job6:21), [14:16](https://ref.ly/Job14:16), [16:7](https://ref.ly/Job16:7). **e.** with an Imv., as an encouragement, implying that the time has come for the exhortation or advice to be followed, [Gn 31:13](https://ref.ly/Gen31:13) עתה קים צא, [Ex 18:19](https://ref.ly/Exod18:19) עתה שְׁמַע בקולי, [Nu 22:11](https://ref.ly/Num22:11), [Dt 2:13](https://ref.ly/Deut2:13), [Is 30:8](https://ref.ly/Isa30:8).

> \[Str6262] † עַתַּי **n.pr.m.** (Ι)εθθει **1\.** of Judah [1 Ch 2:35](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:35) (עַתָּי), v:[36](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:36). **2\.** of Gad [12:11](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:11) **.3\.** [2 Ch 11:20](https://ref.ly/2Chr11:20)

> \[Str6261] † עִתִּי **adj.** timely, ready (Gk. ὡραῖος );—´אִישׁ ע [Lv 16:21](https://ref.ly/Lev16:21) *a man* who is *in readiness.*

> \[Str3282,834,3588] יַעַן:96 prop. **subst.,** purpose, intention, but always used as **prep.** or **conj.,** on account of, because (for יַעֲנֶה, of the form יִצְהָר etc., Sta§ 259 Köii. 403; cf. عَنَى *mean, intend*):—**1\.** as **prep.,** rarely with a subst., † Ez [5:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek5:9) יַעַן תּוֺעֲבוֺתֵיכֶם, [Hg 1:9](https://ref.ly/Hag1:9) יַעַן מָה, v:[9](https://ref.ly/Hag1:9) יַעַן בֵּיתִי; with a ptcp. Ez [36:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:13) יען אמרים *because of men saying* to you (but rd. prob. אָמְרָם, Co Toy Berthol); freq. with inf. c., [1 K 21:20](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:20) יַעַן הִתְמַכֶּרְ *on account of* thy having sold thyself, [Am 5:11](https://ref.ly/Amos5:11), [Is 30:12](https://ref.ly/Isa30:12), [37:29](https://ref.ly/Isa37:29) (\= [2 K 19:28](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:28)) יַעֵן הִתְרַגֶּזְ אֵלַי, [Je 5:14](https://ref.ly/Jer5:14), [7:13](https://ref.ly/Jer7:13), [23:38](https://ref.ly/Jer23:38), [48:7](https://ref.ly/Jer48:7), and oft.. (c. 18 t.) in Ezek., as [5:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek5:7) (rd. יַעַן הַמְרֹֽתְכֶם), [13:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:8), [13:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:22), [25:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek25:3), [25:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek25:6), [25:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek25:8), [25:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek25:12).
> 
>  † **3\.** יַעַן וּבְיַעַן, q.d. *because and by the cause* (that), sq. perh., [Lv 26:43](https://ref.ly/Lev26:43) (H) יען וביען בְּמִשְׁפָּטַי מָאָ֔סוּ, Ez [13:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:10); יַעַן בְּיַעַז sq. inf., Ez [36:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:3). (In all, reckoning ביען(ו)יען once each, 93 t., of which 20 are in 1–2 K, II in Je, 38 in Ez; and only 9 in Gn–2 S.)

> \[Str4617] †מַעֲנֶה **n.m.**:[Pr 15:1](https://ref.ly/Prov15:1) **answer, response**;—abs. ־רַךְ´מ [Pr 15:1](https://ref.ly/Prov15:1) *a gentle answer;* cstr. מַעֲנִה פִּיו v:[23](https://ref.ly/Prov15:23), cf. לָשׁוֺן ´מ [16:1](https://ref.ly/Prov16:1) (i.e. ability to answer); אֱלֽהִים ´מ [Mi 3:7](https://ref.ly/Mic3:7) i.e. *response* for guidance; abs. also [Pr 29:19](https://ref.ly/Prov29:19) *response* \= obedience; [Jb 32:3](https://ref.ly/Job32:3), [32:5](https://ref.ly/Job32:5) *reply,* refutation; sf. לַמַּעֲנֵהוּ [Pr 16:4](https://ref.ly/Prov16:4) \= for its purpose, that for which it *answers* (v. Toy).

> \[Str4616,834,4617] \[מַ֫עַן] **subst.** purpose, intent, only with לְ, in לְמַ֫עַן:271 **prop.** and **conj.** for the sake of, on account of, to the intent or in order that (abbrev. from מַעֲנֶה: cf. מַ֫עַל; Köii. 116\);—with sf. לְמַעֲנִי, לְמַעַנְ, and לְמַעַנְכֶם;—**1\. prep.**:—**a.** [Gn 18:24](https://ref.ly/Gen18:24) wilt thou ... not spare the place למען חמשׁים הציקים *for the sake of* the 50 righteous? [Dt 30:6](https://ref.ly/Deut30:6) חַיֶּיְ ´ל *for thy life's sake*, [1 K 8:41](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:41) the foreigner who comer from afar שְׁמְֶָ ´ל, [Is 43:14](https://ref.ly/Isa43:14) לְמַעַנְכֶם שִׁלַּחְתִּי בָבֶ֫לָה, [45:4](https://ref.ly/Isa45:4), [62:1](https://ref.ly/Isa62:1) ציון ´ל, [63:17](https://ref.ly/Isa63:17) עבדיך ´ל, [65:8](https://ref.ly/Isa65:8), Ez [36:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:22), [36:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:22), [Jb 18:4](https://ref.ly/Job18:4), [Psalm 122:8](https://ref.ly/Ps122:8), [122:9](https://ref.ly/Ps122:9); (´עַבְדִּי ד) לְמַעַן דָּוִד, i.e. for the sake of David's memory, and the promises given to him, † [1 K 11:12](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:12), [11:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:13), [11:32](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:32), [11:34](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:34), [15:4](https://ref.ly/1Kgs15:4), [2 K 8:19](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:19), [19:34](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:34) (\= [Is 37:35](https://ref.ly/Isa37:35)), [20:6](https://ref.ly/2Kgs20:6) (all D2); לְמַעֲנִי (of ´י) *for my own sake,* i.e. to vindicate my name, † [2 K 19:34](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:34) (\= [Is 37:35](https://ref.ly/Isa37:35)), [20:6](https://ref.ly/2Kgs20:6), [Is 43:25](https://ref.ly/Isa43:25), [48:11](https://ref.ly/Isa48:11) לְמַעֲנִי לְמַעֲנִי אֶעֲשֶׁה, so לְמַעַנְ [Dn 9:19](https://ref.ly/Dan9:19), אֲדֹנָי ´ל v:[17](https://ref.ly/Dan9:17); ´י is said (or entreated) to act (שִׁמְ, שְׁמִי ) לְמַעַן שְׁמוֺ i.e. to maintain his reputation, or character, † [Psalm 23:3](https://ref.ly/Ps23:3), [25:11](https://ref.ly/Ps25:11), [31:4](https://ref.ly/Ps31:4), [79:9](https://ref.ly/Ps79:9), [106:8](https://ref.ly/Ps106:8), [143:11](https://ref.ly/Ps143:11), [Je 14:7](https://ref.ly/Jer14:7), [14:21](https://ref.ly/Jer14:21), Ez [20:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:9), [20:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:14), [20:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:22), [20:44](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:44), [Is 48:9](https://ref.ly/Isa48:9); simil. חַסְדְּ ´ל (i.e. to maintain it consistently) † [Psalm 6:5](https://ref.ly/Ps6:5), [44:27](https://ref.ly/Ps44:26), טוּבְ ´ל † [25:7](https://ref.ly/Ps25:7), צִדְקוֺ ´ל † [Is 42:21](https://ref.ly/Isa42:21).
> 
> **b.** *In view of, on account of* (but not expressing causation distinctly, like מִן **2 f,** or מִפְּנֵי), [Dt 3:26](https://ref.ly/Deut3:26) בי לְמַעַנְכֶם ´ויתעבר י *on your account*, [1 K 11:39](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:39) זאֹת ´ל *in view of this,* שׁוֺרֲרַי ´ל *on account of* my watchful foes, † [Psalm 5:9](https://ref.ly/Ps5:9), [27:11](https://ref.ly/Ps27:11), צורריך ´ל [Psalm 8:3](https://ref.ly/Ps8:3), simil. [69:19](https://ref.ly/Ps69:19); [48:12](https://ref.ly/Ps48:12) ([97:8](https://ref.ly/Ps97:8)) משׁפטיך ´ל …ישמח הר ציון *in view of* thy judgments. **c.** Sq. inf. [Gn 18:19](https://ref.ly/Gen18:19) ´לְמַעַן הָבִיא י *for the purpose of* J.'s bringing \= *to the intent that* J. *might* bring, [37:22](https://ref.ly/Gen37:22) הַצִּיל ´ל *in order to rescue,* [50:20](https://ref.ly/Gen50:20), [Ex 1:11](https://ref.ly/Exod1:11), [9:16](https://ref.ly/Exod9:16), [10:1](https://ref.ly/Exod10:1), etc., [Dt 2:30](https://ref.ly/Deut2:30), [6:23](https://ref.ly/Deut6:23), etc., [2 K 10:19](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:19), [Je 7:10](https://ref.ly/Jer7:10), [7:18](https://ref.ly/Jer7:18), [11:5](https://ref.ly/Jer11:5), [50:34](https://ref.ly/Jer50:34) (on הִרגיע, v. עַד **2 a** *b*), Ez [14:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek14:5), [21:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:5), [22:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:6) etc., [36:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:5) (v.p. 176\), \+ oft. (Köiii.595 f.). Once, pleon., לְמַעַן לָמוּג לֵב Ez [21:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:20) (cf. כַּעֲבוּר לְ [1 Ch 19:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr19:3); but rd. prob. הִמּוֺג). **c.** Sq. לאֹ *(a)* אשׁר לאֹ ´ל † [Nu 17:5](https://ref.ly/Num17:5), [Dt 20:18](https://ref.ly/Deut20:18), Ez [31:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:14), [36:30](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:30), [46:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek46:18); *(b)* לאֹ ´ל † Ez [14:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek14:11), [19:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek19:9), [25:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek25:10), [26:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:20), [Zech 12:7](https://ref.ly/Zech12:7), [Psalm 119:11](https://ref.ly/Ps119:11), [119:80](https://ref.ly/Ps119:80), [125:3](https://ref.ly/Ps125:3) (Köiii.574\. Less common than לְבִלְתִּי or מִן with inf., or פֶּ־ן). *Note* **1\.**—לְמַעַן is always *in order that,* never merely *so that* ἐκβατικῶς); but sts., in rhetorical passages, the issue of a line of action, though really undesigned, is represented by it ironically as if it were designed: [Dt 29:18](https://ref.ly/Deut29:18) (v. Dr) ´סְפוֺת וגו ´ל …וְהִתְבָּרֵךְ כִּלְבָבוֺ and he congratulate himself ... *in order to* sweep away the moistened with the dry (i.e. to destroy all together), [Is 30:1](https://ref.ly/Isa30:1), [44:9](https://ref.ly/Isa44:9) יֵבוֺשׁוּ ´בל יראו ובל ידעו ל *in orer that they may be put* to shame, [Je 7:18](https://ref.ly/Jer7:18) הַכְעִיסֵנִי ´ל, v:[19](https://ref.ly/Jer7:19) בּשֶׁת פְנֵיהֶם ´ל, [27:10](https://ref.ly/Jer27:10), [27:15](https://ref.ly/Jer27:15), [32:29](https://ref.ly/Jer32:29), [Ho 8:4](https://ref.ly/Hos8:4) their silver, etc., they have made into idols יִכָרֵת ´ל *in order that* it may be cut off (of course, not the *real* purpose of the idolatry), [Am 2:7](https://ref.ly/Amos2:7), [Mi 6:16](https://ref.ly/Mic6:16): cf. [Psalm 51:6](https://ref.ly/Ps51:6) (v. Comn.) *in order that* thou mightest be just when thou judgest (*sc.* by manifesting thy justice in judgement on my sin). Cf. لِ Qor 28:7 (FlKl. Schr. i. 397 f.).

## I קבב (id: BDB08390)

I. קבב (√ of foll.; cf. Ar. ܛܪܢܐ *arch, dome* (hence *‘al\-cove’*), *vaulted tent,* esp. *tent of honour* GoldziherZMG xlvii (1893\), 74 f. JacobBeduinenleben (2\) 86, 245; Syr. قُبَّةٌ *vault, dome,* ܩܒܳܒܳܐ *id., vaulted tent* Psalm3466 b; so NH קוּבָּה, specif. *lupanar;* Targum קוּבְּתָא *vault* (esp. *vaulted* heavens); perh. also As. *ḳabâbu, sheild*).

> \[Str6894] † קַב **n.\[m.]** ḳab (NH *id.*; Syr. ܩܘܒܬܐ; Talm. קַבָא);—a measure of capacity, BH only dry measure, רֹבַע הַקַּב [2 K 6:25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:25) 1/4 *ḳab*; on size of קַב \= 4 לֽג \= 1/6 סְאָה \= 1/6 הִין \= c. 2 litres v. NowArch. i. 202 f. BenzArch. 182\.

> \[Str6898] † קֻבָּה **n.f.** large vaulted tent;—abs. [Nu 25:8](https://ref.ly/Num25:8) (P) Ke al. Zimri’s *princely tent;* \> Thes al. *large tent* used as *lupanar* (cf. NH).

* **Associated Passages:** Numbers 25:8; 2 Kings 6:25

## קבה (id: BDB08394)

קבה (√ of foll. (cf. DlPr 113 Köii.1:185\): Ar. ܩܒܳܐ *echinus,* i.e. *stomachi pars pelliculata;* Syr. قْبِيتْء *is collect, contain,* water, ܩܒ *cistern;* Eth. ቀበወ፡ *have dropsy;* NH קֵבָה \= BH)

> \[Str6896–97] קֵבָה, sf. קֳבָתָהּ (Köl. c. cf. Ges§ 10 h) **n.f.** stomach, belly;—**1\.** abs. ´הַקּ: fo sacrificial victim, assigned to priest [Dt 18:3](https://ref.ly/Deut18:3), LXX τὸ ἕνυστρον, i.e. *fourth stomach* of ruminants (cf. Dr), Vulgate *ventriculum.* **2\.** sf. of woman, appar. more gen., *belly* [Nu 25:8](https://ref.ly/Num25:8) (P).

* **Associated Passages:** Numbers 25:8; Deuteronomy 18:3


## קָבַל (id: BDB08396)

\[Str6901] † \[קָבַל] **vb. Pi.** receive, take (late) (Aram. loan\-word (and in Aram. denom.) Gerber32, cf. Aram. קְבֵיל(לִ) *in front,* ܡܶܢ ܩܒܘܿܠ *opposite,* B Aram. לָקֳבֵל *before,* O Aram. Palm. לקבל *over against,* Nab. *id., in view of;* also Sab. לקבל *in view of* CISiv, No. 79, 11\. 2\. 4\. 5\. 8, As. *ḳablu, encounter;* verbs are: Sab. קבל *accept,* DHMZMG xxix (1875\), 615; xxx (1876\), 672; Ar. قِبَلَ *be in front, opposite,* قَبَلَ *accept, admit;* Eth. ቀበለ፡, esp. 111\. 2, *go to meet, accept;* NH קָבַל *accuse,* Pi. \= BH; Aram. קַבֵּל, ܩܰܒܶܠ *receive*);—*Pf.* 3 ms. קִבֵּל [Est 4:4](https://ref.ly/Esth4:4) \+ ([9:27](https://ref.ly/Esth9:27) Qr קִבְּלוּ); 3 pl. קִבּלוּ [Ezr 8:30](https://ref.ly/Ezra8:30); *Impf.* 3 ms. sf. וַיְקַנְּלֵם [1 Ch 12:19](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:19) (v:[18](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:18) van d. H.); 3 mpl. וַיְקַבְּלוּ [2 Ch 29:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:16), [29:22](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:22); 1 pl. נְקַבֵל [Jb 2:10](https://ref.ly/Job2:10), [2:10](https://ref.ly/Job2:10); *Imv.* ms. קַבֵּל [Pr 19:20](https://ref.ly/Prov19:20), קַבֶּ־ל [1 Ch 21:11](https://ref.ly/1Chr21:11);—**1\.** *take,* c. acc. rei [2 Ch 29:22](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:22), [Ezr 8:30](https://ref.ly/Ezra8:30); acc. om. [2 Ch 29:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:16), \+ ל reflex. \= choose [1 Ch 21:11](https://ref.ly/1Chr21:11); *receive,* c. acc. Pers. [12:19](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:19) (van d. H. v18). **2\.** *accept,* c. acc. rei [Jb 2:10](https://ref.ly/Job2:10), [2:10](https://ref.ly/Job2:10); מוּסָר ´ק: [Pr 19:20](https://ref.ly/Prov19:20); acc. om. [Est 4:4](https://ref.ly/Esth4:4).**3\.** *accept, assume*, and obligation, sq. cl., [Est 9:23](https://ref.ly/Esth9:23), sq. inf. v:[27](https://ref.ly/Esth9:27) (\+ עַל reflex.). **Hiph.** *Pt.* fpl. מַקְבִּילֽת *shew oppositeness,* i.e. correspond, one to (אֶל) another [Ex 26:5](https://ref.ly/Exod26:5), [36:12](https://ref.ly/Exod36:12) (both P).

> \[Str6904,6905] † \[קְבֹל] **n.\[m.] 1\.** something in front spec. an attacking\-engine (cf. قُبُلٌ *front;* ܐܰܩܒܶܠ, *advance* to attack, so הקביל [Ecclus 12:5](https://ref.ly/Sir12:5)), Ez [26:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:9) מְחִי קָֽבֳלּוֺ the stroke of his *attacking\-engine* (on the form *ḳobolló ,* from קְבֹל for *ḳŭbŭl,* see Ol:§ 169 Köii. 68 f.; and cf. קָֽטֳנִּי from קְטֹן for *ḳŭṭŭn,* קָֽטָבְךָ, קָֽרֶבְכֶם; and on the בֳָ, Ges§ 93 q). **2\.** cstr. קָֽבָל־עָם (*ḳobol*) [2 K 15:10](https://ref.ly/2Kgs15:10), explained formerly as *before* the people, i.e. publicly (cf. BAram קֳבֵל *before*); but the Aram. is surprising and הָעָס needed: rd. כְּיִבְלְעָם *in Ibleam,* with LXX of L (confirming earlier conj. of GrG ii. 1\. 99 StaGi. 575\), Dr Klo Benz Kit al.

* **Associated Passages:** 1 Chronicles 21:11; Esther 9:23; Esther 9:27; Proverbs 19:20; Job 2:10; Sirach 12:5; Esther 4:4; Exodus 36:12; 2 Chronicles 29:16; 2 Kings 15:10; Ezra 8:30; 2 Chronicles 29:22; Exodus 26:5; 1 Chronicles 12:18; Ezekiel 26:9; 1 Chronicles 12:19

## II קדד (id: BDB08411)

> \[Str6936] †קָדְקֹד **n.\[m.]** head, crown of head;—´ק: abs. [Dt 33:20](https://ref.ly/Deut33:20), [Je 2:16](https://ref.ly/Jer2:16), cstr. [Is 3:17](https://ref.ly/Isa3:17) \+; sf. קָדְקֳדוֺ [2 S 14:25](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:25), [Jb 2:7](https://ref.ly/Job2:7); קָדְקֳדֶָךָ [Dt 28:25](https://ref.ly/Deut28:25);—esp. poet, [Gn 49:26](https://ref.ly/Gen49:26)\=[Dt 33:16](https://ref.ly/Deut33:16), [Psalm 7:17](https://ref.ly/Ps7:17), שֵעָר ´ק: [68:22](https://ref.ly/Ps68:22) *hairy crown* or *scalp* (all ‖ראֹשׁ), [Is 3:17](https://ref.ly/Isa3:17), [Je 2:16](https://ref.ly/Jer2:16), [48:45](https://ref.ly/Jer48:45) (‖פֵּאָה), so rd.also [Nu 24:17](https://ref.ly/Num24:17) for וְקַרְקַר (‖ *id.*), v. Ew Di Pat al., [Dt 33:20](https://ref.ly/Deut33:20); in prose only ´מִכַּף רֶגֶל וְעַד־ק: [Dt 28:35](https://ref.ly/Deut28:35), [2 S 14:25](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:25), [Jb 2:7](https://ref.ly/Job2:7).

> \[Str6916] † קִדָּה **n.f.** a spice, usu. **cassia** SyrVer Vulgate Targum cf. DIEx; √ dub.; perh. foreign word); \*— merchan\-dise Ez [27:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:19); ingredient of sacred oil [Ex 30:24](https://ref.ly/Exod30:24) (P):—cf. Dioscor.:De Mater. Med. I.12 (Κασσία\=ἡ κιττώ) TheophrHist. Paint ix. 5 PlinNH xii. 19 (42 ff.); v. also SchrödPh. Spr. 126 LewyFremdvi. 36\.

* **Associated Passages:** Job 2:7; Psalms 7:17; Exodus 30:24; Deuteronomy 33:20; Ezekiel 27:19; 2 Samuel 14:25; Deuteronomy 28:35; Deuteronomy 33:16; Genesis 49:26; Jeremiah 2:16; Jeremiah 48:45; Numbers 24:17; Psalms 68:22; Isaiah 3:17; Deuteronomy 28:25


## קָדַח (id: BDB08414)

\[Str6919] † \[קָדַח] **vb.** be kindled, kindle (NH *id.,* also *bore*, Aram. קְדַח, ܩܕܰܚ *bore, kindle;* Ar. قَدَحَ strike fire (with flint, etc.); Ph. מקדח is *lamp\-lighter* (in temple) acc. To CISI, No.352, i.s);—**Qal 1\.** be kindled: *Pf.* 3 fs.אֵשׁ קָֽדְחָה בְּאַפִּי [Dt 32:22](https://ref.ly/Deut32:22), [Je 15:14](https://ref.ly/Jer15:14), [Je 15:14](https://ref.ly/Jer15:14), [17:4](https://ref.ly/Jer17:4) (rdg.קָֽדְחָה for קְדַחְתֶּם), all of ´י:’s wrath. **2\.** act. *kindle; Inf. cstr.* כִּקְדֹחַ אֵשׁ הֲמָסִים [Is 64:1](https://ref.ly/Isa64:1) (v. \[הֶמֶס]); Pt.קֹדְחֵי אֵשׁ [50:11](https://ref.ly/Isa50:11).

> \[Str6920] † קַדַּ֫חַת **n.f. fever**;—[Dt 28:22](https://ref.ly/Deut28:22), [Lv 26:16](https://ref.ly/Lev26:16).

> \[Str688] † אֶקְדָּח **n.\[m.]** perh. fiery glow, sparkle; אַבְנֵי אֶקְדָּֽת [Is 54:12](https://ref.ly/Isa54:12) i.e. carbuncles (?);—LXX λίθους κπυστάλλό, Vulgate *lapides sculptos* Aq Theod Sym.

* **Associated Passages:** Deuteronomy 28:22; Jeremiah 15:14; Leviticus 26:16; Deuteronomy 32:22; Isaiah 54:12; Isaiah 64:1; Isaiah 50:11; Jeremiah 17:4


## קדם (id: BDB08417)

קדם (√ of foll.; *be before, in front,* cf. As. *ḳudmu front, former time;* Ar. قَدَمَ *precede* (so Eth. ቀደመ፡), IV, v. *be bold and foremost in attack* (קָדַם **1 a**); Sab.קדם *begin* HomChreat, 126, *march forth, advance*, CISiv, No 79, 1\. 9 Sab DenkmNo. 9, 1\.2\.4\.6\.7\.8\.9, (הו)קדמ *front* CISiv, No. 276, 1\.2; Ph.כקדם *as formerly* (?); OAram.קדם *before*, so Palm. Nab.; BAram. Targum קֳדָם *before;* Syr.ܩܕܰܡ *precede;* NH קָדַם *be in front*).

## קָדַר (id: BDB08432)

\[Str6937] †קָדַר **vb.** be dark (*dull\-coloured,* cf. Ar. قَدِ/ُرَ *be dirty* (on د \= ד v. NöZMG xl (1886\), 729\); NH Hiph. (of face) *shew gloom;* TargumJ קְדַר *be dark*);—**Qal** *Pf.* consec. וְקָדַר [Mi 3:6](https://ref.ly/Mic3:6); 1 S. קָדַרְתִּי [Je 8:21](https://ref.ly/Jer8:21), etc.; *Pt.* קֹדֵר [Psalm 35:14](https://ref.ly/Ps35:14) \+; pl. קֹדְרִים [Jb 5:11](https://ref.ly/Job5:11), [6:16](https://ref.ly/Job6:16);—*be dark,* of sky [Je 4:28](https://ref.ly/Jer4:28) (leaden\-coloured, as with clouds, v. Hithp.), of sun and moon [Jo 2:10](https://ref.ly/John2:10), [4:15](https://ref.ly/John4:15); fig. of lack of revelation from ´י:, עֲלֵיהֶם הַיּוֺם ´וְק: [Mi 3:6](https://ref.ly/Mic3:6); of turbid stream [Jb 6:16](https://ref.ly/Job6:16); fig. of mourning (prob. *be squalid,* of neglected person and dress of mourner, cf. [2 S 19:25](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:25)) [Je 8:21](https://ref.ly/Jer8:21), [14:2](https://ref.ly/Jer14:2) (לָאָרֶץ, metaph. of gates); [Jb 5:11](https://ref.ly/Job5:11), [30:28](https://ref.ly/Job30:28) (בְּלֹא חַמָּה), [Psalm 35:14](https://ref.ly/Ps35:14), [38:7](https://ref.ly/Ps38:7), [42:10](https://ref.ly/Ps42:10), [43:2](https://ref.ly/Ps43:2). **Hiph. 1\.** *darken: Pf.* 1 S. consec. וְהִקְדַּרְתִּי Ez [32:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:7) (obj. stars), cf. *Impf.* 1 S. sf. אַקְדִּירֵם עָלֶיךָ [32:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:8). **2\.** *cause to mourn* וָאַקְדִּר עָלָיו לְבָנוֺן [31:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:15). **Hithp.** *Pf.* 3 pl. וְהַשָּׁמַיִם הִתְקַדְּרוּ עָבִים [1 K 18:45](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:45) *and the heavens grew dark with clouds.*

> \[Str6940] † קַדְרוּת **n. f.** darkness, gloom;—אַלְבִּישׁ ´שָׁמַיִם ק: [Is 50:3](https://ref.ly/Isa50:3) (cf. √, [Je 4:28](https://ref.ly/Jer4:28), [1 K 18:45](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:45); ‖שַק).

> \[Str6941] †קְדֹרַנִּית **adv.** as mourners;—[Mal 3:14](https://ref.ly/Mal3:14).

> \[Str6938] †קֵדָר **n.pr.gent.** (*swarthy? black*\-tented?); Κηδαρ: **1\.** tribe of nomads in Arab. desert [Is 21:16](https://ref.ly/Isa21:16), [42:11](https://ref.ly/Isa42:11) (c. vb. fem.), [60:7](https://ref.ly/Isa60:7), [Je 2:10](https://ref.ly/Jer2:10), [49:28](https://ref.ly/Jer49:28), [49:28](https://ref.ly/Jer49:28), Ez [27:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:21); ´בְּנֵי־ק: [Is 21:17](https://ref.ly/Isa21:17); ´אָהֳלֵי ק: [Psalm 120:5](https://ref.ly/Ps120:5), Ct [1:5](https://ref.ly/Song1:5) (made of black goat\-skins JacobBeduinenleben (2\), 41 or black woven stuff DoughtyArab. Des. i. 224 f.; sim. of swarthy hue). **2\.** ancestor of **1,** son of Ishmael, [Gn 25:13](https://ref.ly/Gen25:13) \= [1 Ch 1:29](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:29).—Cf. As. *Ḳidru* COT:[Gn 25:13](https://ref.ly/Gen25:13), PlinNH v. 11 (12\) *Cedrei;* also Sab. tribe\-name קדר Hal623 (cf. DHMZMG xxxvii (1883\), 14\).

> \[Str6939] †קִדְרוֺן **n. pr.** of wady just E. of Jerusalem (Thes. *turbidus*);—´נַחַל ק: [2 S 15:23](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:23), [1 K 2:37](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:37); usu. as place for refuse [1 K 15:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs15:13) \= [2 Ch 15:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr15:16), [2 K 23:6](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:6), [23:6](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:6), [23:12](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:12), [2 Ch 29:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:16), [30:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:14), [Je 31:40](https://ref.ly/Jer31:40); so ´שַׁדְמוֺת ק: [2 K 23:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:4); Κεδρων; cf. RobPhys. Geogr, 87ff. BuhlGeogr. 93 BDPal. 3 (1898\), 94\.

## קדשׁ (id: BDB08437)

קדשׁ (√ of foll.; poss. orig. idea of *separation, withdrawal* (BaudStudien, ii NöLOB Mar. 22, 1879, 361 RS Sem. i. 140; 2nd ed. 150\); NH \= BH; Ph. קדש *holy,* מקדש *sanctuary;* As. *ḳadâšu* II. I, *cleanse* (MeissnSuppl. 84\), also *ḳadištu, hierodule* consecrated to Ištar (cf. AJeremIzdubar 59f.); Ar. قُدْسٌ n.pr.mont. (Nöl. c. RSProph. v., N. 9\); in Ar. otherwise under infl. of Heb., so Eth.; cf. Targum קדשׁ in der. spec. and deriv., Syr. ܩܰܕܶܫ *consecrate,* etc.; Palm. קדש *id.;* Aram. קְדָשָׁא, ܩܕܳܫܳܐ (ear\- or nose\-) *ring,* (orig. *holy* thing, Nöl. c.);—on whole subj. v. Baud Nö RS (reff. above), also HPSPresb. Rev. 1881, 588ff.; diff. fr. חרם v. GFMJu. p. 36\).

[20:14](https://ref.ly/Num20:14) (E), v:[22](https://ref.ly/Num20:22) (P), Ju [11:16](https://ref.ly/Judg11:16), [11:17](https://ref.ly/Judg11:17) (hence RobBR ii. 175, 194 sought it at ‘*Ain Weibeh*, in W. of Arabah); long adobe of Isr. during Exod. ## קָהָה (id: BDB08445)

\[Str6949] † \[קָהָה] **vb.** be blunt, dull (NH *id.;* Aram. קְהָא, ܩܗܳܐ);—**Qal** *Impf.* 3 fpl. תִּקְהֶינָה [Je 31:29](https://ref.ly/Jer31:29): Je 30, Ez [18:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:2) (all of teeth). **Pi.** in sense of **Qal:** *Pf.* 3 ms. קֵהָה [Ec 10:10](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:10) *if the iron be blunt*.

> \[Str5356,5356] † \[קֵהָיוֺן] **n.\[m.]** bluntness (LagBN 201\);—rd. perh. cstr. קִהְיוֺן [Am 4:6](https://ref.ly/Amos4:6) (for נִקְיוֺן; v. נִקָּיוֺן). p.667

* **Associated Passages:** Amos 4:6; Ezekiel 18:2; Ecclesiastes 10:10; Jeremiah 31:29


## קהל (id: BDB08447)

[Nu 16:3](https://ref.ly/Num16:3), [17:7](https://ref.ly/Num17:7), [20:2](https://ref.ly/Num20:2) (P), poss. also [Ex 32:1](https://ref.ly/Exod32:1) (J; most, *unto* Aaron). **2\.** for religious purposes: to erect tent of meeting, acc. loc. [Jos 18:1](https://ref.ly/Josh18:1) (P); hear word of ´י:, c. אֶל loc. [Lv 8:4](https://ref.ly/Lev8:4) (P), ב loc. \+ אֶל Pers. [Je 26:9](https://ref.ly/Jer26:9); for festival, אֶל Pers. [1 K 8:2](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:2) \= [2 Ch 5:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr5:3); לְ loc. > \[Str6953] † קֹהֶ֫לֶת, († קוֺהֶ֫לֶת [Ec 12:8](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:8)) **n.m.**:[Ec 1:2](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:2) collector (of sentences), or (AV RV) preacher (*concionator* Jer.: ἐκκλησιαστής LXX (member of an ἐκκλησία); fem. either of office, Ges§ 122 r De Now Che, cf. סֹפֶרֶת [Ezr 2:55](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:55); خَلِيفَةٌ, *Khalif;* Germ. *Majestät, Excellenz;* or as in Ar. with intensive force, one realizing the idea in its completeness (cf. جَمَّاعَةٌ *great collector*, بَاقِرَةٌ *deep investigator* (formed like קֹהֶלֶת), WAG i. § 233 R. c. v. DrIntr.. 466; RVm *great orator*): hence either *convener,* or *great collector* (of sentences), cf. בַּעֲלֵי אֲסֻפּוֺת [Ec 12:11](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:11), למעא דברי חפץ ´בקּשׁ ק: v:[10](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:10) (but KöEinl. 428 Drl. c. al. *speaker in assembly,* Plumptre *debater*));—´ק: a king in Jerus., son of David, prob. \= Sol., the pseudonym of book of Ec.: [Ec 1:1](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:1), v:[2](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:2) (appos. of אֲנִי), called חכם [12:9](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:9), cf. v:[10](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:10); ´אמר ק: [1:2](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:2), ´אמר הק: [12:8](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:8) \= [7:27](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:27) for MT ´אמרה ק:.

## I קָוָה (id: BDB08462)

\[Str6960] † I. \[קָוָה] **vb.** wait for (prob. orig. *twist, stretch,* then of *tension* of enduring, waiting: As. *ḳu’û* II, I. *wait, ḳû, cord;* Ar. قَوِىَ *be strong,* قُوَّةٌ *strength,* also *strand* of rope; Syr. ܩܰܘܺܝ *endure, remain, await,* ܩ̈ܘܳܝܶܐ *threads,* so Targum קַוִּין spider’s *threads, web*);—**Qal** *Pt.* pl. *those waiting for* (´י:): cstr. ´קוֵי י: [Psalm 37:9](https://ref.ly/Ps37:9); so [Is 40:31](https://ref.ly/Isa40:31) van d. H., and Kt Baer Gi (Qr קוֺיֵי); sf. קִָ\+וי [49:23](https://ref.ly/Isa49:23), קֹוֶיךָ [Psalm 25:3](https://ref.ly/Ps25:3), [69:7](https://ref.ly/Ps69:7); קִוָיו [La 3:35](https://ref.ly/Lam3:35) Qr (Kt קוו, i.e. קֹווֺ). **Pi.** *Pf.* 3 fs. קִוְּתָה [Psalm 130:5](https://ref.ly/Ps130:5); 1 S. קִזּיתִי [Gn 49:18](https://ref.ly/Gen49:18) \+ 5 t., קִוֵּיתִי [Is 5:4](https://ref.ly/Isa5:4), ´וְק: consec. 8:17; sf. קִוִּיתִיךָ [Psalm 25:21](https://ref.ly/Ps25:21); 3 pl. קִוּוּ [Psalm 56:7](https://ref.ly/Ps56:7) \+, etc.; *Impf.* יְקַוֶּה [Mi 5:6](https://ref.ly/Mic5:6), [Jb 7:2](https://ref.ly/Job7:2), juss. יְקַו [Jb 3:9](https://ref.ly/Job3:9), וַיְקַו [Is 5:2](https://ref.ly/Isa5:2), [5:7](https://ref.ly/Isa5:7), etc.; *Imv.* ms. קַזֵה [Ho 12:7](https://ref.ly/Hos12:7) \+; *Inf. abs.* קַוֹּה [Psalm 40:2](https://ref.ly/Ps40:2), קַוֵּה [Je 8:15](https://ref.ly/Jer8:15), [14:19](https://ref.ly/Jer14:19);—**1\.** *wait,* or *look eagerly, for,* c. ל rei [Is 5:7](https://ref.ly/Isa5:7), [59:9](https://ref.ly/Isa59:9), [59:11](https://ref.ly/Isa59:11), [Je 8:15](https://ref.ly/Jer8:15), [13:16](https://ref.ly/Jer13:16), [14:19](https://ref.ly/Jer14:19), [Jb 3:9](https://ref.ly/Job3:9), [6:19](https://ref.ly/Job6:19); לִישׁוּעָֽתְךָ [Gn 49:18](https://ref.ly/Gen49:18); c. acc. rei [La 2:16](https://ref.ly/Lam2:16) (sf.), [Jb 7:2](https://ref.ly/Job7:2), [30:26](https://ref.ly/Job30:26), [Psalm 39:8](https://ref.ly/Ps39:8); obj. rei om. [Is 64:2](https://ref.ly/Isa64:2); sq. inf. [Is 5:2](https://ref.ly/Isa5:2), [5:4](https://ref.ly/Isa5:4), [Psalm 69:21](https://ref.ly/Ps69:21); abs. [Jb 17:13](https://ref.ly/Job17:13); c. acc. ´י: [Is 26:8](https://ref.ly/Isa26:8) (sf.), [Psalm 25:5](https://ref.ly/Ps25:5), [25:21](https://ref.ly/Ps25:21) (sf.), [40:2](https://ref.ly/Ps40:2) (\+ inf. abs.), [130:5](https://ref.ly/Ps130:5), acc. om. v:[5](https://ref.ly/Ps130:5), acc. שִׁמְךָ [Psalm 52:11](https://ref.ly/Ps52:11) (but rd. prob. אֲחַוֶּה, Hi Che Bae al., v. III. חוה); c. ל of ´י: [Is 8:17](https://ref.ly/Isa8:17), [25:9](https://ref.ly/Isa25:9), [25:9](https://ref.ly/Isa25:9), [33:2](https://ref.ly/Isa33:2), [60:9](https://ref.ly/Isa60:9) (but rd. prob. יִקָּווּ *ships shall gather* (II. קוה), so Du CheHpt Di\-Kit Marti, cf. Skinner (also Luzz Gei Oort), [Je 14:22](https://ref.ly/Jer14:22), [Pr 20:22](https://ref.ly/Prov20:22); c. אֶל־אֱלֹהִים [Ho 12:7](https://ref.ly/Hos12:7), ´אֶל־י: [Is 51:5](https://ref.ly/Isa51:5), [Psalm 27:14](https://ref.ly/Ps27:14), [27:14](https://ref.ly/Ps27:14), [37:34](https://ref.ly/Ps37:34). **2\.** *lie in wait for,* sq. נַפְשִׁי [Psalm 56:7](https://ref.ly/Ps56:7); sq. ל Pers. [Psalm 119:95](https://ref.ly/Ps119:95) (\+ inf. purpose). **3\.** *wait* (linger) *for* c. ל of man [Mi 5:6](https://ref.ly/Mic5:6) (‖יְיַחֵל).

Köii. l. 40 Anm. 2\);—abs. קַו Ez [47:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:3) \+ [Zc 1:16](https://ref.ly/Zech1:16) Qr (Kt קוה), קָו [Is 34:17](https://ref.ly/Isa34:17) \+; cstr. קַו [2 K 21:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs21:13) \+;—measuring\-*line* (מִדָּה ´ק: [Je 31:39](https://ref.ly/Jer31:39)): [1 K 7:23](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:23) (Qr; Kt קוה, v. infr.) \= [2 Ch 4:2](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:2), Ez [47:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:3); for marking off a possession inland [Is 34:17](https://ref.ly/Isa34:17) (fig.); esp. עַל´נְטָה ק: in building, [Jb 38:5](https://ref.ly/Job38:5) (fig. of earth), [Zc 1:16](https://ref.ly/Zech1:16) (יִנְּטֶה; ‖יִבָּנֶה), so ´יְצָא ק: [Je 31:39](https://ref.ly/Jer31:39) (Qr; Kt קיה); ´נָטָה ק: of designing idol [Is 44:13](https://ref.ly/Isa44:13); marking off for destruction, עַל´נָטָה ק: [2 K 21:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs21:13), [Is 34:11](https://ref.ly/Isa34:11) (קַרתֹהוּ), ´ק :´נ: [La 2:8](https://ref.ly/Lam2:8); so מִשְׁפָּט לְקָ֔ו וְשַמְתִּ֫י [Is 28:27](https://ref.ly/Isa28:27).—קַוָּם [Psalm 19:5](https://ref.ly/Ps19:5) (AV *their line.* fig. of their domain) rd. prob. קֹלָם *their sound,* Capp. Ol Che and now most. ## קוּט (id: BDB08474)

\[Str5354,6962,6990,5354,6985] † \[קוּט] **vb.** feel a loathing (‖ form of קוּץ q.v.);—**Qal** *Impf.* 1 s. אָקוּט בְּדוֺר [Psalm 95:10](https://ref.ly/Ps95:10) *I felt a loathing at* the generation. **Niph.** *Pf.* 2 mpl. consec. וּנְקֹטֹתֶם בִּפְנֵיכֶם Ez [20:43](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:43) *ye shall feel loathing against your faces* (at yourselves), c. ב rei, so [36:31](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:31) (עַל rei); metapl. (as if from קטט) 3 pl. consec. וְנָקֹטוּ בִפְנֵיהֶם Ez [6:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek6:9) (אֶל rei, for עַל, \+ ל rei, for which Co בְּ Krae בְּ or עַל); also 3 fs. נָֽקְטָה נַפְשַׁי בְּ [Jb 10:1](https://ref.ly/Job10:1) (as if from נקט\*; on both forms v. Ges§ § 67 dd, 72 dd). **Hithpō‘l.** *id. Impf.* 1 s. אֶתְקוֺטָט בְּ [Psalm 139:21](https://ref.ly/Ps139:21) (We אֶתְקוֺמֵם); abs. וָאֶתְקוֺטָטָה [119:158](https://ref.ly/Ps119:158).—Ez [16:47](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:47) v. קָט; [Jb 8:14](https://ref.ly/Job8:14) v. foll. p. 881

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 95:10; Ezekiel 16:47; Psalms 119:158; Job 10:1; Ezekiel 36:31; Job 8:14; Ezekiel 6:9; Psalms 139:21; Ezekiel 20:43


## קוֺט (id: BDB08475)

\[Str6962] † \[קוֺט] dub. **vb.intrans.,** assumed mng. **break, snap** (supposed to be akin to Ar. قَطَّ *cut, cut off, pare, trim* \[whence Buhl assumes √ קטט]), to account for יְקוֺט [Jb 8:14](https://ref.ly/Job8:14), either as **Qal** *Impf.* 3 ms. whose hope *snappeth;* or as **n.\[m.]** \= *fragile thing* (‖בֵּית עַכָּבִישׁ; cf. Di Bu); prob. crpt.; Du Beer rd. קוּרִים Bu conj. קֻרֵי קַיִט \[Aramaism for קַיִץ ´ק:].

* **Associated Passages:** Job 8:14

## קוּם (id: BDB08479)

\[Str6965] קוּם:628 **vb.** arise, stand up, stand;—(NH in der. spec. and deriv.; Ph. Hiph. Pt. and מקם *place;* Ar. قَامَ *stand, rise,* Eth. و *stand, stop;* Sab. מקם, *place,* HomChrest. 127; Aram. קוּם, ቆመ፡ \= BH, so OAram. Nab. קום; Palm. Aph. אקים *erect statue*; cf. prob. As. *kumu* (*k* \= כ), *place, dwelling, kum, kêmu, in place of,* and perh. *kaiamânu, enduring, constant* (v. כִּיּוּן); Thes cp. also Sam. ܩܳܡ, *live,* cf. esp. Targum Pa.);—**Qal**:460 *Pf.* 3 ms. קָם [1 S 17:48](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:48) \+, וְקָאם consec. [Ho 10:14](https://ref.ly/Hos10:14) (Ges§ 72 p); 2 ms. קַמְתָּ [2 S 12:21](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:21), etc.; *Impf.* 3 ms. יָקוּם [Ex 21:19](https://ref.ly/Exod21:19), juss. יָקֻם [Gn 27:31](https://ref.ly/Gen27:31), (לָח)וְיָ֫קָם [Jb 22:28](https://ref.ly/Job22:28) (Ges§ 109 h Dr§ 152 (3\)); וַיָּ֫קָם [Gn 4:8](https://ref.ly/Gen4:8) \+, etc.; *Imv.* ms. קוּם [Gn 13:17](https://ref.ly/Gen13:17) \+, fpl. קֹמְנָה [Is 32:9](https://ref.ly/Isa32:9), etc.; *Inf. abs.* קוֺם [Je 44:29](https://ref.ly/Jer44:29); *cstr.* קָוּם [Am 5:2](https://ref.ly/Amos5:2) \+, etc.; *Pt.* \> fs. קָמָה [Mi 7:6](https://ref.ly/Mic7:6); mpl. קָמִים [2 S 18:31](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:31) \+, הַקּוֺמִים [2 K 16:17](https://ref.ly/2Kgs16:17) (Ges§ 72 p), etc.;—**1\.** *arise:* מִתַּחְתָּיו [Ex 10:23](https://ref.ly/Exod10:23) (E). Specif.: **a.** after lying down (sleep, sickness, mourning, etc.), [1 S 3:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam3:6), [3:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam3:8), [2 S 12:21](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:21), [Ex 21:19](https://ref.ly/Exod21:19), [Gn 19:33](https://ref.ly/Gen19:33), [19:35](https://ref.ly/Gen19:35), [31:17](https://ref.ly/Gen31:17), [Ru 3:14](https://ref.ly/Ruth3:14), [Ec 12:4](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:4) \+ 50 t.; fr. lying dead, קַיָּקָם עַל־רַגְלָיו [2 K 13:21](https://ref.ly/2Kgs13:21), cf. > \[Str3360] †יְקַמְעָם **n.pr.m.** (\= יָ֫קֶם עָם acc. to Ol:§ 277 i; meaning then *may kinsman establish,* cf. GrayProp. N. 46 f. 59\);—a Levite [1 Ch 23:19](https://ref.ly/1Chr23:19), [24:23](https://ref.ly/1Chr24:23), Ικεμιας,Ιοκομ ([24:23](https://ref.ly/1Chr24:23)), etc., LXXL Ιακαμιας.

> \[Str3361] †יָקְמְעָם **n.pr.loc** (perh. \= יְקָמְעָם i.e. יָ֫קָם עָ Ol:§ 277k, 3; \= *let the people* be *established ?* cf. GrayProp. N. 218\);—in N. Isr. [1 K 4:12](https://ref.ly/1Kgs4:12), Λουκαμ, LXXL Ουκαμ; poss. \= mod. *Tel Ḳaimûn*, N. of Megiddo, BuhlGeogr. 210; city of refuge in Ephr. [1 Ch 6:53](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:53) ( \= קִבְצַיִ \[q.v.] [Jos 21:22](https://ref.ly/Josh21:22)), Ικααμ, Ιεκμααν, etc.

> \[Str6969] קוֺנֵן v. קין

> \[Str6970] †קוֺעַ **n.pr.gent.** named with Bab., Chald., Assyr., ´שׁוֺעַ וְק:(פְּקוֺד וְ) Ez [23:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:23) *Šô‘a and Ḳô‘a*; identif. by DlPa 235 with As. *Sutû, Kutû* (abbrev. *Su* (? v. שׁוֺעַ) and \[by infer.] *Ku*), E. of Tigris, on border of Elam and Media; cf. COTEz [23:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:23) DrHast. iii, KOA; but קוֺעַ \= Kutû now doubted by WklAltor, Forsch. ii. 2 (1899\), 54

> \[Str6971] †\[קוֺף] **n.\[m.]** ape, so Vrss, LXX πίθηκοι (om. LXXB in K) (foreign word; prob. \= Skr. *kapi, id.;* Egypt. as loan\-word gôfë WMMAs.u. Eur. 95, *gi’f* ErmanZMG xlvi (1892\), 121; Gk. κῆβος,κῆπος is of Eg. orig. acc. to LewyFremdw.6\);—pl. קֹפִים [1 K 10:22](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:22) \= קוֺפִים [2 Ch 9:21](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:21), brought to Sol. \[from SE.] by Phoenician fleet; cf. Ar. prov. *‘donum regionis Jemen simiae sunt,’* FreytagProvv. iii. 3160\.

> \[Str8622] †\[תְּקוּפָה] **n.f** coming round, circuit;—cstr. תְּקוּפַת הַשָּׁנָה [Ex 34:22](https://ref.ly/Exod34:22) (JE), adv., *at the circuit* (completion) *of the year,* so הַשָּׁנָה ´לִת: [2 Ch 24:33](https://ref.ly/2Chr24:33); \= pl. cstr. לִתְקֻפוֺת הַיָּמִים [1 S 1:20](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:20); sg. sf. of finished *circuit* of sun [Psalm 19:7](https://ref.ly/Ps19:7) (opp. מוֺצָאוֺ; cf. of moon, בתקופתו [Ecclus 43:7](https://ref.ly/Sir43:7)).

* **Associated Passages:** 2 Chronicles 24:33; Sirach 43:7; 1 Samuel 1:20; Exodus 34:22; Psalms 19:7


## I קוּץ (id: BDB08500)

\[Str6973] †I. \[קוּץ] **vb.** feel a loathing, abhorrence, sickening dread (‖ √of קוט; Targum קוּץ *id.;* for connex. of meanings cf. Aram. קְנַט *loathe,* ܩܢܰܛ *fear*);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 fs. קָ֫צָה [Nu 21:5](https://ref.ly/Num21:5); 1 s. קַ֫צְתִּי [Gn 27:46](https://ref.ly/Gen27:46); *Impf.* 3 ms. וַיָּ֫קָץ [Nu 22:3](https://ref.ly/Num22:3), [1 K 11:25](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:25) (but v. infr.); 2 ms. juss. תָּקֹץ [Pr 3:11](https://ref.ly/Prov3:11); 1 s. וָאָקֻץ [Lv 20:23](https://ref.ly/Lev20:23); 3 mpl. וַיָּקֻצוּ [Ex 1:12](https://ref.ly/Exod1:12); *Pt.* קָץ [Is 7:16](https://ref.ly/Isa7:16);—**1\.** *feel a loathing* at, *abhor*, c. ב rei [Nu 21:5](https://ref.ly/Num21:5) (JE), [Gn 27:46](https://ref.ly/Gen27:46) (P), [Pr 3:11](https://ref.ly/Prov3:11) (‖מָאַס); c. ב Pers. [Lv 20:23](https://ref.ly/Lev20:23) (H ; subj. ´י:); so [1 K 11:25](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:25), but Kit (after SyrVer, cf. LXX) prop. וַ֫יָּצֶק (√ צוק **Hiph.** *distress*). **2\.** *feel a sickening dread*, c. מִפְּנֵי Pers. [Ex 1:12](https://ref.ly/Exod1:12) (J), [Nu 22:3](https://ref.ly/Num22:3) (E), [Is 7:16](https://ref.ly/Isa7:16). **Hiph.** *Impf.* 1 pl. sf. נְקִיצֶנָּה [Is 7:6](https://ref.ly/Isa7:6) (sf. of Judah) *let us cause her sickening dread*, but weak, rd. נְצִיקֶנָּה (√ צוק) Thes Che Du Gr Marti.— Other **Hiph.** forms v. קיץ.

* **Associated Passages:** Genesis 27:46; Exodus 1:12; Leviticus 20:23; Proverbs 3:11; 1 Kings 11:25; Numbers 21:5; Isaiah 7:6; Isaiah 7:16; Numbers 22:3
* **Associated Articles:** II קיץ (ID: `BDB08554@BDBHebrewLexicon`)

## I קוּר (id: BDB08505)

\[Str6979] † I. \[קוּר] **vb.** bore, dig (Ar. قَارَ (و) *cut a round hole in, scoop out*);—**Qal** *Pf.* 1 s. קַרְתִּי [2 K 19:24](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:24) *I have dug* (sc. a well)\= [Is 37:25](https://ref.ly/Isa37:25) (Meinh conj. כָּרִתִי).—**Hiph., Pilp.,** v. I, II. קרר.

> \[Str4726] † מָקוֺר **n.m.**:[Zc 13:1](https://ref.ly/Zech13:1) spring, fountain (app. Orig. *well*);—abs. ´מ: [Zc 13:1](https://ref.ly/Zech13:1), [Pr 25:26](https://ref.ly/Prov25:26); cstr. מְקוֺר [Je 2:13](https://ref.ly/Jer2:13) \+ , מְקֹר [Lv 12:7](https://ref.ly/Lev12:7), [20:18](https://ref.ly/Lev20:18); sf. מְקוֺרוֺ [Ho 13:15](https://ref.ly/Hos13:15), etc.;—**1\.** *spring* of water : **a;** fig., of ´י:, מַיִם ´מ: חַיִּים [Je 2:13](https://ref.ly/Jer2:13), [17:13](https://ref.ly/Jer17:13), cf. חַיִּים ´מ: [Psalm 36:10](https://ref.ly/Ps36:10); חַיִּים ´מ: (more gen.), [Pr 10:11](https://ref.ly/Prov10:11), [13:14](https://ref.ly/Prov13:14), [14:27](https://ref.ly/Prov14:27), [16:22](https://ref.ly/Prov16:22) \+ [18:4](https://ref.ly/Prov18:4) (so rd. for חָכְמָה ´מ: LXX Heb Codd Toy). **b.** fig. of purification Ez [13:1](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:1). **c.** מָקוֺר מָשְׁחָת [Pr 25:26](https://ref.ly/Prov25:26) (fig.; \+ מַעְיָן). **d.** fig. of source of life and vigour [Ho 13:15](https://ref.ly/Hos13:15), [Je 51:36](https://ref.ly/Jer51:36); of a nation’s orig. source, stock [Psalm 68:27](https://ref.ly/Ps68:27) (Kay Che, of temple); source of joy [Pr 5:18](https://ref.ly/Prov5:18) (fig. of wife; ‖אֵשֶׁת). **2\.** fig. of eye, מְקוֺר דַּמְעָה, [Je 8:23](https://ref.ly/Jer8:23). **3\.** *source* of menstruous blood, דָּמֶיהָ ´מְ: [Lv 20:18](https://ref.ly/Lev20:18), so מְקֹרָהּ v:[18](https://ref.ly/Lev20:18) (H). **4\.** \= *flow* of blood after child\-birth דָּמֶיהָ ´מְ: [12:7](https://ref.ly/Lev12:7) (P).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 36:10; Leviticus 12:7; Leviticus 20:18; Jeremiah 51:36; Proverbs 5:18; Proverbs 13:14; Proverbs 10:11; Proverbs 18:4; Jeremiah 8:23; Proverbs 25:26; Proverbs 16:22; 2 Kings 19:24; Jeremiah 17:13; Hosea 13:15; Psalms 68:27; Ezekiel 13:1; Isaiah 37:25; Jeremiah 2:13; Proverbs 14:27; Zechariah 13:1

## קָטַף (id: BDB08523)

\[Str6998] † \[קָטַף] **vb.** pluck off (twigs, etc.), or out (NH\=BH; Ar. قَطَفَ *pluck* grapes; Targum קְטַף, Syr. ܩܛܰܦ; cf. As *ḳatâpu, appar. pluck off*);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms.קָטָף Ez [17:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:4) *he* \[i.e. eagle, in fig.] *plucked off* twigs; 2 ms. וְקָטַפְתָּ֫ [Dt 23:26](https://ref.ly/Deut23:26) (ears of grain, בְּיָדֶָךָ); *Impf.* 1 S אֶקְטֹף Ez [17:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:22) (subj. ´י:; acc. \+מִן *from*); *Pt.* mpl. הַקֹּטְפִים [Jb 30:4](https://ref.ly/Job30:4) *they who pluck out* the mallow. **Niph.** *Impf.* 3 ms. יָקָּטֵב [Jb 8:12](https://ref.ly/Job8:12) *it is not plucked off.*

* **Associated Passages:** Ezekiel 17:22; Job 30:4; Deuteronomy 23:26; Ezekiel 17:4; Job 8:12


## I קטר (id: BDB08524)

I. קטר (√ of foll.; cf. As. *ḳutru* (with ת) *smoke;* Talm. Targum קִיטְרָא, קוּטְרָא (thick) *smoke;* Ar. قَتَرَ *smoke* (said of fire); usu. *exhale odour* (esp. of roast meat); As. *ḳutrinnu, incense\-offering;* Sab. מקטר *censer* CISiv. Nos.26, 1\. 6; 30, 1\.4\. Eth. ቅትኣሬ፡: *incense;* Egypt. *ḳatalθa* Bondi74 ff. *kataruti* WMMAs. u. Eur. 97 \= קְטֹרֶת, קְטָרוֺת; NH \[קָטַר] *smoke* (said of incense)).

קין (√ of foll.; cf. Ar. قَانَ *fit together, fabricate (make artificially), forge* (cf. WetzstSyr.Dreschtafel (1873\) 297\), whence قَيْنٌ *worker in iron,* As. *ḳinai* (MeissnZa viii (1893\), 82\), Targum קֵינָאָה, Syr. ܩܰܝܢܳܝܳܐ, Palm. קיניא (pl.) *metal\-worker,* cf. BH תּוּבַל קַיִן; also Ar. قَيْنَةٌ *slave\-girl,* and *woman\-singer, lute\-player* (fr. skill); cf. Eth. ቅኒ፡ *song, singing,* Syr. ܩܺܝܢܬܳܐ, *hymn, elegy;* Ar. vb. ## I קִיץ (id: BDB08553)

\[Str6974] † I. \[קִיץ] **vb. Hiph.** awake (‖ form of יקץ; NH in Hiph. (rare));—*Pf.* 3 ms. הֵקִיץ [2 K 4:31](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:31), Ez [7:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek7:6); 2 ms. וַהֲקיצ֫וֺתָ consec. [Pr 6:22](https://ref.ly/Prov6:22), etc.; *Impf.* 1 S. אָקִיץ [Pr 23:35](https://ref.ly/Prov23:35); 3 mpl. יָקִ֫יצוּ [Je 51:39](https://ref.ly/Jer51:39) \+; *Imv.* ms. הָקִ֫יצָה Hb [2:19](https://ref.ly/Hab2:19) \+; mpl. הָקִ֫יצוּ [Is 26:19](https://ref.ly/Isa26:19), [Jo 1:5](https://ref.ly/John1:5); *Inf. cstr.* הָקִיץ [Psalm 17:15](https://ref.ly/Ps17:15), [73:20](https://ref.ly/Ps73:20); *Pt.* מֵקִיץ [1 S 26:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:12);—*shew signs of waking, awake:* **1\.** from sleep, **a.** [1 S 26:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:12), [Is 29:8](https://ref.ly/Isa29:8), [29:8](https://ref.ly/Isa29:8), [Psalm 3:6](https://ref.ly/Ps3:6), [73:20](https://ref.ly/Ps73:20), [139:18](https://ref.ly/Ps139:18), [Pr 6:22](https://ref.ly/Prov6:22) (doubtful line, v. Toy). **b.** of ´י: [Psalm 44:24](https://ref.ly/Ps44:24) *awake* \[fr. sleep] to activity, so [35:23](https://ref.ly/Ps35:23) (c. ל rei), [59:6](https://ref.ly/Ps59:6) (c. inf. purpose). **c.** fr. ecstatic sleep of proph. [Je 31:26](https://ref.ly/Jer31:26). **2\.** fr. sleep of death, c. neg. [2 K 4:31](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:31), [Je 51:39](https://ref.ly/Jer51:39), [51:57](https://ref.ly/Jer51:57), [Jb 14:12](https://ref.ly/Job14:12); of resurrection [Is 26:19](https://ref.ly/Isa26:19), [Dn 12:2](https://ref.ly/Dan12:2) **3\.** from stupor (of drunkenness) [Jo 1:5](https://ref.ly/John1:5), [Pr 23:35](https://ref.ly/Prov23:35). **4\.** of inanimate thing Hb [2:19](https://ref.ly/Hab2:19); cf. בָּא הַקֵּץ הֵקִיץ אֵלָיִח Ez [7:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek7:6) *the end cometh, it hath awaked unto thee* (Co del. הֵקִיץ, but the word\-play favours it).—[Psalm 17:15](https://ref.ly/Ps17:15) is put by Thes Ol Hup Du al. sub **1 a**; Ew De Che sub **2**; we sub **1 b ;** Calv Now: from night of distress and helplessness, and so Bae (psalmist representing Isr.).

* **Associated Passages:** Jeremiah 31:26; Jonah 1:5; Daniel 12:2; Psalms 3:6; Isaiah 26:19; Psalms 139:18; Ezekiel 7:6; Proverbs 23:35; Jeremiah 51:39; Psalms 17:15; Job 14:12; Psalms 35:23; 2 Kings 4:31; Psalms 59:6; Psalms 73:20; Isaiah 29:8; Jeremiah 51:57; Psalms 44:24; 1 Samuel 26:12; Habakkuk 2:19; Proverbs 6:22


## II קיץ (id: BDB08554)

II. קיץ (√ of foll.; usu. taken as \= I. קיץ but connex. not clear; cf. Ar. قَيْظٌ *vehement heat of summer, late summer* (WeSkizzen iii, 90\), قَاظَ *be vehemently hot;* Sab. קיטַ *summer* MordtmHim. Inschr. 71; NH קַיִץ \= BH, so Targum קַיְטָא Syr. ܩܰܝܛܳܐ; Oaram. (Zinj.) כיחא *summer*).

## I קָלַע (id: BDB08590)

\[Str7049] † I. \[קָלַע] **vb.** sling, hurl forth (Thes cp. Ar. قَلِعَ *sit insecurely* (v. also Frä224, Socin in Buhl Lex 13\), *waver,* whence قِلْعٌ *sail* (loan\-wd. Frä1. c.), JAram. קַלְעָא *id.,* BH NH קֶלַע *curtain;* thence *hurl, sling;* but this perh. denom. (Socinl. c.) fr. قُلَّاعَةٌ *loosened clod,* cf. Syr.ܩܽܘܠܳܥܳܐ *id.* (Nö in Fräl. c.); Ar. مِقْلَعٌ *sling,* Eth መቅልዕ፡: *id.;* Targum קְלַע vb. *sling,* Syr. n. ܩܶܠܥܳܐ *sling*);—**Qal** *Pt.* קֹלֵעַ בָּאֶבֶן אֶ־ל Ju [20:16](https://ref.ly/Judg20:16) *one slinging* (*with*) *a stone at* a hair; ´קוֺ: c. acc. Pers. [Je 10:18](https://ref.ly/Jer10:18) *I will sling forth* the inhabitants. **Pi.** *Impf.* 3 ms. וַיִקַלַּע [1 S 17:49](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:49) *and he slang* (sc. the stone); sf. יְקַלְּעֶנָּה [25:29](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:29) *he shall sling away* (life of enemy).

קמח (√ of foll.; As. *ḳamû,* perh. *crush, grind; kêmu* (\= *ḳemû ?*), *flour*; NH \= BH, so Targum קִמְחָא, Syr. ܩܰܡܚܳܐ; Vulg. Ar. قَمْحٌ *wheat;* Eth. ቀምሕ: *produce, fruit, vegetables;* cf. Egypt. *kamāḥ*, Bondi77, *ḳmḥ*, a kind of bread, ErmanZMG xivi (1892\), 120\).

H. v:[40](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:40)); kept in a) כַּד [1 K 17:12](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:12), [17:14](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:14), [17:16](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:16); made by grinding [Is 47:2](https://ref.ly/Isa47:2); צֶמַחבְּלִי ´ק: [Ho 8:7](https://ref.ly/Hos8:7).

(Pun.) מקנא, *property* \[in cattle]; As. *ḳanû, gain, acquire*, MeissnSuppl. 85; Ar. قَنَا (و, ي) *acquire, procure*; Sab. קני *acquire, possess*, CISiv,no.89, 5\.6, קני n. *property* Idib,no.3, 8, 29, 3; ## I קָצַע (id: BDB08667)

> \[Str7102] † I. \[קְצִיעָה] **n.f.** cassia, a *powdered* bark, like cinnamon (hence Gk. κασία, Lat. *casia*, LewyFremdw.37\);—pl. פָצִיעוֺת (מֹר וַאֲהָלוֺת) [Psalm 45:9](https://ref.ly/Ps45:9) (Che now תּוּצַק *are shed*, v. inEncy. Bib. CASSIA, n.).

> \[Str4741] † \[מַקָצֻעָה] **n.\[f.]** scraping tool, used in fashioning idols;—pl. בַּמַּקָצֻעוֺת [Is 44:13](https://ref.ly/Isa44:13).

## II קָרָא (id: BDB08697)

\[Str7122] II. \[קָרָא]:137 **vb.** encounter, befall (‖ form of קָרָה, q.v.; SI4 לקרת *to meet, opposite*; Ar. قَرَأَ *put part to part;* Min. קרא *set opposite*, HomChrest. 127\);—**Qal**:131 *Pf.*:5 3 ms. sf. קְרָאַנִי [Je 13:22](https://ref.ly/Jer13:22), [Jb 4:2](https://ref.ly/Job4:2); 3 fs. קָרָאת [Dt. 31:29](https://ref.ly/Deut31:29); [Je 44:23](https://ref.ly/Jer44:23), etc; *Impf.*:4 3 ms. יִקְרָא [Gn 49:1](https://ref.ly/Gen49:1), sf. יִקְרָאֶנוּ [Gn 42:4](https://ref.ly/Gen42:4); 3 fpl. וַתִּקְרֶאנָה [Lv 10:19](https://ref.ly/Lev10:19); [Ex 1:10](https://ref.ly/Exod1:10) rd. 3 fs. sf. תִּקְרָאֵנוּ (Sam Vrss Di Ges§ 47 k SoKau Baen); *inf. cstr.*:121 לקְרַאת (Ges§ 19 k Ol:§ 137\), [Gn 15:10](https://ref.ly/Gen15:10) \+, etc.; *Pt.* fpl. sf. קרְאִתַיִךְ [Is 51:19](https://ref.ly/Isa51:19);—**1\.** *meet, encounter*, only inf. cstr. לִקְרַאת oft.\= prep. *toward, against* (Köii. l, 317\), almost always c. acc. Pers., after vb. of motion, [Is 7:3](https://ref.ly/Isa7:3), [Gn 19:1](https://ref.ly/Gen19:1), [24:17](https://ref.ly/Gen24:17), Ju [4:18](https://ref.ly/Judg4:18), [4:22](https://ref.ly/Judg4:22), [1 K 2:8](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:8) \+; in war, battle, Ju [7:24](https://ref.ly/Judg7:24), [20:25](https://ref.ly/Judg20:25), [20:31](https://ref.ly/Judg20:31), [1 S 4:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:1), [1 K 20:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:27) \+, after עָרַךְ [2 S 10:9](https://ref.ly/2Sam10:9), [10:10](https://ref.ly/2Sam10:10), [10:7](https://ref.ly/2Sam10:7) \+, [Psalm 35:3](https://ref.ly/Ps35:3) v. I. סגר **2 b**; ´לְחַזֵּק לִבָּם לִק: הַמִּלְחָמָה [Jos 11:20](https://ref.ly/Josh11:20) *to harden their heart to encounter the war* with Isr.; after הִנֵּה (without vb. of motion), † [1 S 10:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:10), [2 S 15:32](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:32), [16:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam16:1), [1 K 18:7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:7), [Pr 7:10](https://ref.ly/Prov7:10); in constr. praegn. ´וַיִשְמַח לִק: Ju [19:3](https://ref.ly/Judg19:3) *he rejoiced to meet him,* \+ [1 S 6:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:13) (LXX We Dr al.); ´חרד לִק: [2 K 10:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:15), נצב [Ex 5:20](https://ref.ly/Exod5:20), [7:15](https://ref.ly/Exod7:15) [Nu 22:34](https://ref.ly/Num22:34), etc.; redund. ´לִק :´יִקָּרֶה י: [Nu 23:3](https://ref.ly/Num23:3); implic. of *helping*, [Is 21:14](https://ref.ly/Isa21:14), [Psalm 59:6](https://ref.ly/Ps59:6) (fig.); נְחָשִׁים ´לִק: [Nu 24:1](https://ref.ly/Num24:1) he did not go *to encounter* (in expectation of) *signs of divination;* in metaph. [Is 14:9](https://ref.ly/Isa14:9), אֱלֽהֶיְ ´לִק: [Am 4:12](https://ref.ly/Amos4:12) **2\.** fig. *befall* c. acc. Pers., bad sense [Gn 42:4](https://ref.ly/Gen42:4), [42:38](https://ref.ly/Gen42:38), [Dt 31:29](https://ref.ly/Deut31:29), [Je 13:22](https://ref.ly/Jer13:22), [44:23](https://ref.ly/Jer44:23), [Is 51:19](https://ref.ly/Isa51:19), [Jb 4:14](https://ref.ly/Job4:14), [Lv 10:19](https://ref.ly/Lev10:19); of war [Ex 1:10](https://ref.ly/Exod1:10) (v. supr.) in gen., [Gn 49:1](https://ref.ly/Gen49:1). **Niph.** *meet unexpectedly; Pf.* 3 ms. נִקְרָא, c. עַל Pers. [Ex 5:3](https://ref.ly/Exod5:3); *Impf.* 3 ms. וַיִּקָּרֵא, c. לִפְנֵי Pers. [2 S 18:9](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:9); *Pf.* [20:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:1) c. שָׁם, *chanced to be there; Impf.* of bird’s nest [Dt. 22:6](https://ref.ly/Deut22:6) (לִפְנֵי); *Inf. abs.* נִקְרֹא v. קרה. **Hiph.** *Impf.* 2 ms. וַחַקְרֵא [Je 32:23](https://ref.ly/Jer32:23) *thou didst cause* all this evil *to befall* them (acc. Pers. et rei).

## I קָרֵב (id: BDB08699)

\[Str7126] I. \[קָרֵב], קָרַב:279 **vb.** come near, approach (NH *approach, be offered;* As. *ḳarâbu*, *approach*; Ar. قَرِبَ, قَرُبَ *be near, approach;* so Eth ቀርበ: Sab. קרב *approach* a woman (sexually, v. **1 a** infr.), DHMHofmus. No.6\.1\.2\., but also gen. דַ רחק וקרב  *he who is far and he who is near* CIS iv, no. 95, 7, 8 cf. SabDenkmNo. 12, 9, 10; Aram. קְרֵב, ܩܪܶܒ, *approach*; also, \= *oblation,* Sab. קרבן DHMZMG xxx (1876\), 672 Ar. قُرْبَانٌ, O Aram. קרבו, Targum קוּרְבָּנָא, Syr. ܩܽܘܪܒܳܢܳܐ; cf. [Est 4:7](https://ref.ly/Esth4:7), [6:13](https://ref.ly/Esth6:13), [Dn 10:14](https://ref.ly/Dan10:14) (ל Pers.) ; ´עֵת וָפֶגַע יִק: אֶת־כֻּלָּם [Ec 9:11](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:11); Subj. מִקְרֶה [2:14](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:14), cf. v:[15](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:15). **Niph.** *Pf.* 3 ms נִקְרָה [Ex 3:18](https://ref.ly/Exod3:18); 1 S. נִקְרֵיתִי [2 S 1:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam1:6); *Impf.* 3 ms יִקָּרֶה [Nu 23:3](https://ref.ly/Num23:3), וַיִּקָּר v:[4](https://ref.ly/Num23:4), v:[16](https://ref.ly/Num23:16); 1 S אִקָּרֶה v:[15](https://ref.ly/Num23:15) ;—**1\.** *encounter, meet* without pre\-arrangement, usu. of ´י: (God): c. עַל Pers. [Ex 3:18](https://ref.ly/Exod3:18) (J), אֶל Pers. [Nu 23:4](https://ref.ly/Num23:4), [23:16](https://ref.ly/Num23:16) לִקְרָתִי ´י :´יִקּ: v:[3](https://ref.ly/Num23:3) (all JE); obj. ´י: (om) v:[15](https://ref.ly/Num23:15).**2\.** *chance to be present,* ב loc., [2 S 1:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam1:6) (\+Inf.abs נִקְרֹא). **Hiph. 1\.** of ´י: *cause* ( the right thing good fortune) *to occur,* c. לִפְנֵי *Pf.* 3 ms.הִקְרָה [Gn 27:20](https://ref.ly/Gen27:20) *Imv.* ms. הַקְרֵה [24:12](https://ref.ly/Gen24:12) (both J). **2\.** of Isrel, *Pf.* 2 mpl. cones וְהִקְרִיתֶם לָכָם עָרִים [Nu 35:11](https://ref.ly/Num35:11) ye shall (*caus* cities *to occur* rightly *for yourselves,* i.e.) *select* cities *as suitable*.

> \[Str7137] † \[קָרֶה] **n.\[m.]** chance, accident ;—cstr מִקְּרֵה לַיְלַה [Dt 23:11](https://ref.ly/Deut23:11) *by reason of the chance of the night* (i.e.nocturnal pollution, cf. [Lv 15:16](https://ref.ly/Lev15:16)).

> \[Str7147] † \[קְרִי] **n.\[m.]** opposition, contrariness;—only קֶָרִי(בְּ) in Lv 26, as adv. modifier of הָלַךְ (in fig. sense), \+ עִם Pers., of Isr.’s relation to ´י:: תֵּלְכוּ עִמִּי קָרִי [Lv 26:21](https://ref.ly/Lev26:21) *if ye walk with me contrariwise,* so v:[23](https://ref.ly/Lev26:23), בְּקָרִי v:[27](https://ref.ly/Lev26:27), v:[40](https://ref.ly/Lev26:40); ´י: subj בַּקֶָּרִי וְהָלַכְתי עִמָּכֶם v:[24](https://ref.ly/Lev26:24), so v:[41](https://ref.ly/Lev26:41) and (´בַּחֲמַת ק: *in wrath of opositioin*) v:[28](https://ref.ly/Lev26:28).

> \[Str7157,7154] † קִרְיַת יְעָרִים **n.pr.loc.** πόλ(ε)ις Ιαρειμ\[ν], Καριαθιαρειμ, etc. (*city of forests*);—city of the Gibeonites [Jos 9:17](https://ref.ly/Josh9:17), assigned to Judah [Jos 15:60](https://ref.ly/Josh15:60) cf. Ju [18:12](https://ref.ly/Judg18:12), [18:12](https://ref.ly/Judg18:12), on border of Benj. [Jos 15:9](https://ref.ly/Josh15:9) (יְעָרִים הַר v:[10](https://ref.ly/Josh15:10), LXX πόλιν Ιαρειν, v. יְעָרִים), [18:14](https://ref.ly/Josh18:14), assigned to Benj. v:[28](https://ref.ly/Josh15:28) (יְעָרִים om. by text. err. before עָרִים, v. LXX); near Beth Shemesh [1 S 6:21](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:21), long the abode of ark [7:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam7:1), [7:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam7:2), [1 Ch 13:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr13:5), [13:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr13:6), [2 Ch 1:4](https://ref.ly/2Chr1:4); named in genealogical list [1 Ch 2:50](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:50), [2:52](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:52), [2:53](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:53); named also [Ne 7:29](https://ref.ly/Neh7:29) \= עָרִים ´ק: [Ezr 2:25](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:25) (read יְעָרִים); c. art. הַיְּעָרִים ´ק: [Je 26:20](https://ref.ly/Jer26:20); abbreviated (in poet.) (שְדֵ־י) ָ\+יעַר [Psalm 132:6](https://ref.ly/Ps132:6); called also קִרְיַת־בַּעַל (Καριαθβααλ) [Jos 15:60](https://ref.ly/Josh15:60), [18:14](https://ref.ly/Josh18:14) \+ v:[15](https://ref.ly/Josh15:15) (where read for יְעָרִים ´ק:, so LXX Di Benn Steuern); this abbrev. into בַּעֲלָה, [15:9](https://ref.ly/Josh15:9), [15:10](https://ref.ly/Josh15:10) \[(Ιε)βααλ], also v:[11](https://ref.ly/Josh15:11), v:[29](https://ref.ly/Josh15:29), and מִבַּעֲלֵי יְהוּדָה [2 S 6:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam6:2) (rd. ´בַּעַל יְה:, or ´בַּעֲלַת יְה:), ‖בַּעֲלָתָה [1 Ch 13:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr13:6) (v. on these II. בַּעֲלָה). Identif. uncertain; RobBR ii. 11 conj. *Kiryat el\-‘Enab,* 8 m. \+ W. of Jerusalem (cf. Καριαθιαρειμ LagOnom. 271\); *‘Erma* (HendersonPal. 85, 112, 210 ConderSurvey Mem. iii. 43 ff.), cf. GASmGeogr. 225 f. BuhlGeogr. 166 f..

## קָרַח (id: BDB08730)

\[Str7139] † I \[קָרַח] **vb.** make bald, or a baldness, usu. (by mourners) for the dead, cf. RS Sem. i. 306, 2nd ed. 324 (Arab. Parallels) (NH *id.* Hiph. *shew baldness;* Ar. قَرَحَ *wound, make sores,* قُرْحَةٌ *whiteness in face* of horse (but قَرَعَ *be bald,* أَقْرَعُ *bald*); Eth. ቀርሐ፡ *make bald* (rare); Aram. קְרַח, ܩܪܰܚ, chiefly deriv. (and der. Meanings); cf. Sin. n.pr.m. קרחה, קרחו; MI3, 21, 24, 25 n.pr.loc. קרחה);—**Qal** *Impf.* 3 mpl. יִקְרְחוּ קָרְחָה בְּראִשָׁם [Lv 21:5](https://ref.ly/Lev21:5) Qr (\> Kt יקרחה; ‖יְגַלֵָּחוּ cf. [Dt 14:1](https://ref.ly/Deut14:1)); *Imv.* fs. קָרְחִי וָגֹ֫זִּי עַל [Mi 1:16](https://ref.ly/Mic1:16) *make a baldness... for.* **Niph.** *Impf.* 3 ms. יִקָּרֵחַ לָהֶם [Je 16:6](https://ref.ly/Jer16:6) men *shall* not *make themselves bald for them*. **Hiph.** *Pf.* 3 pl. consec. וְהִקְרִיחוּ אֵלַיִח קָרְחָה Ez [27:31](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:31) *and they shall make a baldness* (cf. **Qal**) *for thee*. **Hoph.** *Pt.* כָּל־ראִשׁ מֻקְרָח Ez [29:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:18) *every head made bald* (by carrying load).

## קָרַם (id: BDB08740)

\[Str7159] † \[קָרַם] **vb.** spread or lay something over (NH *id. form a crust,* so Hiph. יקרים על [Ecclus 43:20](https://ref.ly/Sir43:20); Aram. קְרַם, ܩܪܰܡ, *incrust, overlay*);—**Qal** *Pf.* 1 s. וְקָרַמְתִּ֫י עֲלֵיכֶם עוֺר Ez [37:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:6) *I will spread skin over you* (i.e. the dry bones); *Impf.* 3 ms. וַיִּקְרַם עֲלֵיהֶם עוֺר v:[8](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:8) (so Gi van d. H.; Baer וַיִּקְרָם, of which conflicting explan. Ges§ § 9 u, 29 i), indef. Subj. instead of pass.; but rd. **Niph.** וַיִּקָּ֫רֶם SyrVer (cf. Vulgate) Co Berthol Toy Krae.

* **Associated Passages:** Ezekiel 37:8; Sirach 43:20; Ezekiel 37:6

## קָרַס (id: BDB08746)

\[Str7164] † \[קָרַס] **vb.** bend down, stoop, crouch (intrans.) (cf. perh. Ar. قُرْصٌ *disc, round cake;* cp. NH קַרְסוֺל, קַרְצוֺל, Targum קַרְסוּלָּא, קַרְצוּלָא all *ankle*);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 pl. קָֽרְסוּ [Is 46:2](https://ref.ly/Isa46:2); *Pt.* קֹרֵס v:[1](https://ref.ly/Isa46:1) (both \+ כָּרַע); of vanquished gods of Babylon.

> \[Str7165] † \[קֶ֫רֶס] **n.\[m.]** hook, on edge of curtains of tabern., matching the loops (לֻלָאֹת, v. \[לוּלַי]); pl. קְרָסִים [Ex 26:6](https://ref.ly/Exod26:6), [26:11](https://ref.ly/Exod26:11) \= [36:13](https://ref.ly/Exod36:13), [36:18](https://ref.ly/Exod36:18), [26:33](https://ref.ly/Exod26:33); cstr. קַרְסֵי זָהָב v:[6](https://ref.ly/Exod26:6) \= [36:13](https://ref.ly/Exod36:13), נְחשֶׁת ´ק: [26:11](https://ref.ly/Exod26:11) \= [36:18](https://ref.ly/Exod36:18); sf. קְרָסָיו [35:11](https://ref.ly/Exod35:11) \= [39:33](https://ref.ly/Exod39:33).

> \[Str7166,7165] † \[קַרְסֹל] **n.**\[**f.** AlbrZAW xvi (1896\), 77] ankle (cf. LXX Vulgate [2 S 22:37](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:37); \= ל \+ קֶרֶס v. Köii. 1, 121; pl. sf. קַסֻלָּי [2 S 22:37](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:37) \= [Psalm 18:37](https://ref.ly/Ps18:37)).

> \[Str7026] † קֵרֹס **n.pr.m.** post\-ex., [Ezr 2:44](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:44), Καδης, A Κηραος; \= קֵירֹס [Ne 7:47](https://ref.ly/Neh7:47), Κειρα(ς), LXXL (both) Κορες.

## I קָשָׁה (id: BDB08782)

\[Str7185] I. \[קָשָׁה] **vb.** be hard, severe, fierce (NH קָשָׁה *be severe;* Ar. قَسَا (ي) *be hard, dry,* III. *endure, struggle against, severity;* Aram. קְשָׁא, ܩܫܳܐ *be hard, difficult;* J Aram. קְשִׁי, קַשְׁיָה adj. *hard*);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 fs. קָֽשְׁתָה [1 S 5:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam5:7), קָשָׁתָה [Gn 49:7](https://ref.ly/Gen49:7); *Impf.* 3 ms. יִקְשֶׁה [Dt 1:17](https://ref.ly/Deut1:17), [15:18](https://ref.ly/Deut15:18), וַיִּ֫קֶשׁ [2 S 19:44](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:44);—**1\.** *be hard, difficult* [Dt 1:17](https://ref.ly/Deut1:17) (of a legal case; מִן compar.). **2\.** *be hard, severe,* יָדוֺ ´ק: עָלֵינוּ[1 S 5:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam5:7) (of ´י:, in judgement); בְעֵינֶָךָ ´יִק:. [Dt 15:18](https://ref.ly/Deut15:18) *it shall not be hard in thine eyes* (seem a severe trial, an injustice); of wrath [Gn 49:7](https://ref.ly/Gen49:7) (poem in J; ‖עָז); of fierce words [2 S 19:44](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:44). **Niph.** *Pt.* נִקְשֶׁה [Is 8:21](https://ref.ly/Isa8:21) *hardly bestead, hard pressed.* **Pi.** *Impf.* 3 fs. וַתְּקַשׁ בְּלִדְתָּהּ [Gn 35:16](https://ref.ly/Gen35:16) (E) *she made hard in her bearing* (had severe labour). **Hiph.** *Pf.* 3 ms. הִקְשָׁה [Ex 13:15](https://ref.ly/Exod13:15) \+, etc.; *Impf.* 3 ms. וַיֶּקֶשׁ [2 Ch 36:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr36:13); 3 mpl. וַיַּקְשׁוּ [Je 7:26](https://ref.ly/Jer7:26) \+, etc.; *Inf. cstr.* sf. בְּהַקְשֹׁתָהּ [Gn 35:17](https://ref.ly/Gen35:17); *Pt.* מַקְשֶׁה [Pr 28:14](https://ref.ly/Prov28:14), [29:1](https://ref.ly/Prov29:1);—**1\.** *make difficult, difficulty:* הִקְשָׁה לְשַׁלְּחֵנוּ [Ex 13:15](https://ref.ly/Exod13:15) Pharaoh *made difficulty about sending us away;* of travail [Gn 35:17](https://ref.ly/Gen35:17) (E; as **Pi.**); הִקְשִׁיתָ לִשְׁאוֺל [2 K 2:10](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:10) *thou hast made hard to ask,* asked a hard thing. **2\.** *make severe, burdensome,* yoke imposed by king [1 K 12:4](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:4) \= [2 Ch 10:4](https://ref.ly/2Chr10:4). **3\. a.** *make hard, stiff, stubborn,* fig. of obstinacy: וַיַּקְשׁוּ אֶת־עָרְפָּם [2 K 17:14](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:14) *they stiffened their neck,* so [Je 7:26](https://ref.ly/Jer7:26), [17:23](https://ref.ly/Jer17:23), [19:15](https://ref.ly/Jer19:15), [Dt 10:16](https://ref.ly/Deut10:16), [2 Ch 30:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:8), [36:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr36:13), [Ne 9:16](https://ref.ly/Neh9:16), [9:17](https://ref.ly/Neh9:17), [9:29](https://ref.ly/Neh9:29), [Pr 29:1](https://ref.ly/Prov29:1); אַל־תַּקְשׁוּ לְבַבְכֶם [Psalm 95:8](https://ref.ly/Ps95:8), cf. [Pr 28:14](https://ref.ly/Prov28:14); subj. ´י:, ´אַקְשֶׁה אֶת־לֵב פ: [Ex 7:3](https://ref.ly/Exod7:3) (P), acc. אֶת־רוחוֺ [Dt 2:30](https://ref.ly/Deut2:30), **b.** *shew stubborness* [Jb 9:4](https://ref.ly/Job9:4) (c. אֶל Pers. \[´י:]).

> \[Str7186] † קָשֶׁה **adj.** hard, severe;—abs. ´ק: [Ex 18:26](https://ref.ly/Exod18:26) \+; cstr. [32:9](https://ref.ly/Exod32:9) \+; fs. קָשָׁה [Dt 26:6](https://ref.ly/Deut26:6) \+; cstr. קְשַׁת [1 S 1:15](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:15); mpl. קָשִׁים [2 S 3:39](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:39); cstr. קְשֵׁי Ez [2:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek2:4), [3:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:7); fpl. קָשׁוֺת [Gn 42:7](https://ref.ly/Gen42:7), [42:30](https://ref.ly/Gen42:30);—**1\.** *hard, difficult,* of a legal question (דָּבָר) [Ex 18:26](https://ref.ly/Exod18:26) (E). **2\. a.** *severe,* of battle [2 S 2:17](https://ref.ly/2Sam2:17), wind [Is 27:8](https://ref.ly/Isa27:8), servitude (עֲבֹדָה) [1 K 12:4](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:4) \= [2 Ch 10:4](https://ref.ly/2Chr10:4), [Dt 26:6](https://ref.ly/Deut26:6), [Is 14:3](https://ref.ly/Isa14:3), [Ex 1:14](https://ref.ly/Exod1:14), [6:9](https://ref.ly/Exod6:9) (both P); as n.coll. הִרְאִיתָ עַמְּךָ קָשָׁה [Psalm 60:5](https://ref.ly/Ps60:5) *thou hast made thy people see severe things;* קְשֵׁה יוֺם [Jb 30:25](https://ref.ly/Job30:25) i.e. one whose time (life) is hard, so [1 S 1:15](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:15) (reading קְשַׁת יוֺם for רוּחַ ´ק:, LXX Th We Dr al.); *severe, rough,* of lord (אֲדֹנִים) [Is 19:4](https://ref.ly/Isa19:4), cf. [2 S 3:39](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:39) (c. מִן comp. \[מִמֶּנּוּ? v. HPS]); הָלוֺח וְקָשָׁה עַ־ל Ju [4:24](https://ref.ly/Judg4:24) the hand of the sons of Israel went on *being more and more severe* upon Jabin; *fierce, relentless,* of ´י:’s sword [Is 27:1](https://ref.ly/Isa27:1) (in fig.); *rough, rude* [1 S 25:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:3); *severe things,* of language [Gn 42:7](https://ref.ly/Gen42:7), [42:30](https://ref.ly/Gen42:30) (E; c. דִּדֶּר), [1 S 20:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:10), [1 K 12:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:13) \= [2 Ch 10:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr10:13) (all c. עָנָה); וְאָנֹכִי שָׁלוּחַ אֵלַיִח קָשָׁה [1 K 14:6](https://ref.ly/1Kgs14:6) *I am commissioned with a severe (message) unto thee* (Ges§ 121d. N.2 DaSynt. §§ 75 (d), 80\); חָזוּת קָשָׁה [Is 21:2](https://ref.ly/Isa21:2). **b.** *fierce, intense, vehement,* of קִנְאָה Ct [8:6](https://ref.ly/Song8:6) (‖עַזָּה; cf. [Gn 49:9](https://ref.ly/Gen49:9)). **3\.** עַם־קְשֵׁה־עֹרֶף *a people stiff of neck, stubborn,* [Ex 32:9](https://ref.ly/Exod32:9), [33:3](https://ref.ly/Exod33:3), [33:5](https://ref.ly/Exod33:5), [34:9](https://ref.ly/Exod34:9) (all JE), [Dt 9:6](https://ref.ly/Deut9:6), [9:13](https://ref.ly/Deut9:13); עָרְפְּךָ הַקָּשֶׁה [31:27](https://ref.ly/Deut31:27); cf. דַּרְכָּם הַקָּשָׁה Ju [2:19](https://ref.ly/Judg2:19); קְשֵׁי פָנִים Ez [2:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek2:4) (\+ חִזְקֵי־לֵב), קְשֵׁא־לֵב [3:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:7) (\+ חִזְקֵי־מְצַח); ´ק: alone \= *stubborn* [Is 48:4](https://ref.ly/Isa48:4).

> \[Str7190] † קְשִׁי **n.** \[**m.**] stubbornness;—cstr. [Dt 9:27](https://ref.ly/Deut9:27).

## קָשַׁר (id: BDB08794)

[Dt 6:8](https://ref.ly/Deut6:8), etc.; *Impf.* 3 ms. וַיִּקְשֹׁר [1 K 15:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs15:27) \+, וַיִּקְשָׁ־ר [2 K 15:30](https://ref.ly/2Kgs15:30), etc.; *Imv.* ms. sf. קָשְׁרֵם [Pr 3:3](https://ref.ly/Prov3:3) \+; *Pt. act.* pl. קשְׁרִים [2 S 15:31](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:31) \+; *pass.* fs. קְשׁוּרָה [Gn 44:30](https://ref.ly/Gen44:30), [Pr 22:15](https://ref.ly/Prov22:15); pl. קְשֻׁרִים [Gn 30:42](https://ref.ly/Gen30:42);—**1\.** *bind:* **a.** lit., c. acc. rei \+ עַל [Gn 38:28](https://ref.ly/Gen38:28) (J), [Je 51:63](https://ref.ly/Jer51:63), \+ ב, [Jos 2:18](https://ref.ly/Josh2:18), [2:21](https://ref.ly/Josh2:21) (JE); *bind, confine,* c. acc. רֵים [Jb 39:10](https://ref.ly/Job39:10) (\+ בְּתֶלֶם), acc. of crocod. ## I קשׁשׁ (id: BDB08797)

I. קשׁשׁ (√ of foll.; cf. Targum קְשַׁשׁ *be old* (orig. *be dried up*)?, Syr. ܩܰܫ *be old;* J Aram. קַשִּׁישׁ *old;* Palm. קשישא name of office, perh. *elder;* NH קַשׁ *stubble, straw* (Löwp. 160\), so Syr. ܩܶܫܳܐ, Targum קַשָּׁא ([Ex 15:7](https://ref.ly/Exod15:7), [Is 40:24](https://ref.ly/Isa40:24)); Ar. قَشٌّ is loan\-word Frä137).
